Now comparing against the baseline plan006-prolog-sta-s42, the exec mode achieves significantly higher prove rate at 83% versus 50%, but with much lower ISR at 0.0308 versus 0.5. This indicates the exec solver excels at generating valid, compilable programs but doesn't capture as much of the structural information from the traces.

I need to update the registry with these results for plan006-prolog-exec-s42, then report the findings. The checkpoint cleanup is a bit ambiguous—the task warned against deleting it during eval, but now that evaluation is complete, freeing that 31GB would be helpful. However, since the instructions don't explicitly authorize cleanup after eval, I'll leave it as is.
